\title{Melanie-MASTERS (human masters) — Nimzo-Indian}
\author{Melanie (Deep\_Vision)}
\date{}
\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, White: \texttt{cxd5}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{cxd5} (\texttt{c4d5}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0026\#2147} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=57, QAP obj=82]: the master played \texttt{exd5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pe4}; cross-type roles: our \emph{Q}d1 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{p}b7 as their \emph{N}g1; transferred move \texttt{c4d5} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0030\#207} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=67, QAP obj=58]: the master played \texttt{b3}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Bc1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pb2} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{N}f3 as their \emph{R}c1, our \emph{P}f2 as their \emph{R}h1; transferred move \texttt{c1d2}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0005\#1225} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=72, QAP obj=54]: the master played \texttt{Bc2}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pd4} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bb3} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{b}c8 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{R}a1 as their \emph{N}b1; transferred move \texttt{d4c5}. Synthesis: 1 retrieved analog transferred to \texttt{cxd5} (summed QAP objective 82, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m12}
\end{figure}
